        Idea:
Implement Phase 1 (Kernel) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 1 scope: events.jsonl with plain sha256 hash chain (prev_hash + canonical_event_json, no HMAC); plan.json hash pinning; gate above dispatch as a single function checking active_run.json existence + plan.json hash match + events.jsonl chain integrity + incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command; active_run.json pointer at the project root. The runner becomes dumb; the gate is decorated/composed above runner dispatch. This is additive: existing OrchestratorDefinition JSON manifests and RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. No new step kinds yet (those are Phase 2). No produces-with-checks (Phase 3). No DSL (Phase 4). No new lifecycle verbs in this phase — that comes in Phase 5; for now, exercise the kernel through existing entry points so it can be verified end-to-end. Honor-based with strong logging — accidental-edit tripwire only. Single-host. Storage paths under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/<slug>/. Add focused tests for: hash-chain append + verify; plan-hash pin + mismatch rejection; gate-rejects-non-canonical-command with the exact recovery error; gate is called from every dispatch entry point. Migration is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13) so respect the current pack architecture.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

    "FLAG-002 (open, minor): plan_hash uses canonical-JSON re-serialization and tolerates whitespace-only edits to plan.json.     "Gate must run STRICTLY BEFORE _prepare_project_request() and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run() at every entry point \u2014 assert this ordering by filesystem checks (no <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ created on rejection).",
    "Recovery strings 'artagents abort --project <slug>' and 'artagents next --project <slug>' are emitted verbatim per SD-011 even though those CLI verbs do not exist until Phase 5. Do not substitute Phase-1-actionable wording.",
    "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is a NEW sibling env var introduced in Phase 1 (the design doc names only ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID). Plan justifies it because SD-018 mirroring requires a step id in the child. Keep the constant in artagents/core/task/env.py and document in the docs footnote.",
    "Step retries with the same step_id reuse <step_dir>/ flat (mkdir parents=True, exist_ok=True). Iterations directory is Phase 3 scope; do not introduce <step_dir>/iterations/NNN/ in Phase 1.",

    "TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS is unchanged in Phase 1 \u2014 core/task/ lives under existing core/ package. SD-016's orchestrate/verify additions belong to Phase 4. Do not edit TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS or tests/test_doctor_setup.py.",

    "Subprocess env propagation into child code-step launchers is Phase 2 scope; Phase 1 only ships the child_subprocess_env() helper used directly in the e2e smoke."

FLAG-002 stays open as documented (canonical-JSON hashing, not raw bytes); flag it in code review but do not change scope without user confirmation. Recovery strings forward-reference Phase 5 verbs that don't exist yet (artagents abort, artagents next) \u2014 emit them verbatim per SD-011, do not substitute. New env var ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is in-scope for Phase 1 (justified in plan assumptions because SD-018 mirroring needs a step id in the child); document it in the Phase 1 footnote so it is visible at Phase 5 review time.",

        Idea:
Implement Phase 1 (Kernel) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 1 scope: events.jsonl with plain sha256 hash chain (prev_hash + canonical_event_json, no HMAC); plan.json hash pinning; gate above dispatch as a single function checking active_run.json existence + plan.json hash match + events.jsonl chain integrity + incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command; active_run.json pointer at the project root. The runner becomes dumb; the gate is decorated/composed above runner dispatch. This is additive: existing OrchestratorDefinition JSON manifests and RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. No new step kinds yet (those are Phase 2). No produces-with-checks (Phase 3). No DSL (Phase 4). No new lifecycle verbs in this phase — that comes in Phase 5; for now, exercise the kernel through existing entry points so it can be verified end-to-end. Honor-based with strong logging — accidental-edit tripwire only. Single-host. Storage paths under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/<slug>/. Add focused tests for: hash-chain append + verify; plan-hash pin + mismatch rejection; gate-rejects-non-canonical-command with the exact recovery error; gate is called from every dispatch entry point. Migration is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13) so respect the current pack architecture.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

  "meta_commentary": "Phase 1 is purely additive and runs entirely in-process \u2014 no infra, secrets, or human approvals required. Execute strictly in plan order: kernel modules (T1\u2013T4) \u2192 unit tests (T5) \u2192 env-contract change with regression check (T6\u2013T7) \u2192 pipeline wiring (T8) \u2192 defensive re-checks one runner at a time (T9\u2013T10) with intermediate test runs \u2192 dispatch coverage (T11) \u2192 e2e smoke (T12) \u2192 docs (T13) \u2192 full sweep (T14). The single highest-risk file is artagents/core/project/run.py: keep the env-set branch local to prepare_project_run/finalize_project_run, never touch the env-unset code path, and re-run tests/test_project_runs.py immediately after T6 \u2014 if it fails, the env contract has leaked into the standalone path and must be reverted before proceeding. The four gate entry points all share one ordering invariant: gate runs BEFORE _prepare_project_request and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run; verify by filesystem assertion that <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ does not appear after rejection. command_for_argv must produce the exact canonical form that plan.json command strings are authored against, including the --project <slug> token; this is the contract that makes hand-authored plans work in T12. FLAG-002 stays open as documented (canonical-JSON hashing, not raw bytes); flag it in code review but do not change scope without user confirmation. Recovery strings forward-reference Phase 5 verbs that don't exist yet (artagents abort, artagents next) \u2014 emit them verbatim per SD-011, do not substitute. New env var ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is in-scope for Phase 1 (justified in plan assumptions because SD-018 mirroring needs a step id in the child); document it in the Phase 1 footnote so it is visible at Phase 5 review time.",

        Idea:
Implement Phase 1 (Kernel) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 1 scope: events.jsonl with plain sha256 hash chain (prev_hash + canonical_event_json, no HMAC); plan.json hash pinning; gate above dispatch as a single function checking active_run.json existence + plan.json hash match + events.jsonl chain integrity + incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command; active_run.json pointer at the project root. The runner becomes dumb; the gate is decorated/composed above runner dispatch. This is additive: existing OrchestratorDefinition JSON manifests and RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. No new step kinds yet (those are Phase 2). No produces-with-checks (Phase 3). No DSL (Phase 4). No new lifecycle verbs in this phase — that comes in Phase 5; for now, exercise the kernel through existing entry points so it can be verified end-to-end. Honor-based with strong logging — accidental-edit tripwire only. Single-host. Storage paths under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/<slug>/. Add focused tests for: hash-chain append + verify; plan-hash pin + mismatch rejection; gate-rejects-non-canonical-command with the exact recovery error; gate is called from every dispatch entry point. Migration is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13) so respect the current pack architecture.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

    "FLAG-002 (open, minor): plan_hash uses canonical-JSON re-serialization and tolerates whitespace-only edits to plan.json.     "Gate must run STRICTLY BEFORE _prepare_project_request() and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run() at every entry point \u2014 assert this ordering by filesystem checks (no <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ created on rejection).",
    "Recovery strings 'artagents abort --project <slug>' and 'artagents next --project <slug>' are emitted verbatim per SD-011 even though those CLI verbs do not exist until Phase 5. Do not substitute Phase-1-actionable wording.",
    "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is a NEW sibling env var introduced in Phase 1 (the design doc names only ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID). Plan justifies it because SD-018 mirroring requires a step id in the child. Keep the constant in artagents/core/task/env.py and document in the docs footnote.",
    "Step retries with the same step_id reuse <step_dir>/ flat (mkdir parents=True, exist_ok=True). Iterations directory is Phase 3 scope; do not introduce <step_dir>/iterations/NNN/ in Phase 1.",
    "is_in_task_run(slug) must guard on BOTH TASK_RUN_ID_ENV being set AND (slug is None OR matches TASK_PROJECT_ENV). The slug-mismatch check inside prepare_project_run is a redundant defense for direct callers that bypass the gate.",
    "When attached_to_task_run is truthy, finalize_project_run must NOT write child run.json and must NOT upsert the cumulative runs.json. mirror_hype_artifacts must still run; verify it lands files under <step_dir>/produces/ rather than a sibling child runs/<id>/.",
    "tests/test_project_runs.py must pass WITHOUT EDITS \u2014 env-unset code path must be byte-identical to today.",
    "Inactive gate must be fully transparent: no active_run.json + no ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID + no --project \u21d2 GateDecision(active=False) and ZERO events written. Confirm in tests.",
    "Cursor derivation in Phase 1 = count(step_completed events). Richer semantics (iterations/items/nested) are Phase 2-3 only.",
    "TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS is unchanged in Phase 1 \u2014 core/task/ lives under existing core/ package. SD-016's orchestrate/verify additions belong to Phase 4. Do not edit TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS or tests/test_doctor_setup.py.",
    "RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} in artagents/core/orchestrator/schema.py is unchanged. No new step kinds (code/attested/nested) in Phase 1.",
    "No HMAC, keyed crypto, .index.db, daemon, web server, or shared CAS \u2014 SD-029. Hash chain is plain sha256.",
    "command_for_argv produces the canonical form plan.json command strings must match exactly (including --project <slug> token). Document this contract in code comment near the helper or in the docs footnote.",
    "Subprocess env propagation into child code-step launchers is Phase 2 scope; Phase 1 only ships the child_subprocess_env() helper used directly in the e2e smoke."

        Idea:
Implement Phase 1 (Kernel) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 1 scope: events.jsonl with plain sha256 hash chain (prev_hash + canonical_event_json, no HMAC); plan.json hash pinning; gate above dispatch as a single function checking active_run.json existence + plan.json hash match + events.jsonl chain integrity + incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command; active_run.json pointer at the project root. The runner becomes dumb; the gate is decorated/composed above runner dispatch. This is additive: existing OrchestratorDefinition JSON manifests and RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. No new step kinds yet (those are Phase 2). No produces-with-checks (Phase 3). No DSL (Phase 4). No new lifecycle verbs in this phase — that comes in Phase 5; for now, exercise the kernel through existing entry points so it can be verified end-to-end. Honor-based with strong logging — accidental-edit tripwire only. Single-host. Storage paths under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/<slug>/. Add focused tests for: hash-chain append + verify; plan-hash pin + mismatch rejection; gate-rejects-non-canonical-command with the exact recovery error; gate is called from every dispatch entry point. Migration is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13) so respect the current pack architecture.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

    "Gate must run STRICTLY BEFORE _prepare_project_request() and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run() at every entry point \u2014 assert this ordering by filesystem checks (no <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ created on rejection).",
    "Recovery strings 'artagents abort --project <slug>' and 'artagents next --project <slug>' are emitted verbatim per SD-011 even though those CLI verbs do not exist until Phase 5. Do not substitute Phase-1-actionable wording.",
    "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is a NEW sibling env var introduced in Phase 1 (the design doc names only ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID). Plan justifies it because SD-018 mirroring requires a step id in the child. Keep the constant in artagents/core/task/env.py and document in the docs footnote.",
    "Step retries with the same step_id reuse <step_dir>/ flat (mkdir parents=True, exist_ok=True). Iterations directory is Phase 3 scope; do not introduce <step_dir>/iterations/NNN/ in Phase 1.",
    "is_in_task_run(slug) must guard on BOTH TASK_RUN_ID_ENV being set AND (slug is None OR matches TASK_PROJECT_ENV). The slug-mismatch check inside prepare_project_run is a redundant defense for direct callers that bypass the gate.",
    "When attached_to_task_run is truthy, finalize_project_run must NOT write child run.json and must NOT upsert the cumulative runs.json. mirror_hype_artifacts must still run; verify it lands files under <step_dir>/produces/ rather than a sibling child runs/<id>/.",
    "tests/test_project_runs.py must pass WITHOUT EDITS \u2014 env-unset code path must be byte-identical to today.",
    "Inactive gate must be fully transparent: no active_run.json + no ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID + no --project \u21d2 GateDecision(active=False) and ZERO events written. Confirm in tests.",
    "Cursor derivation in Phase 1 = count(step_completed events). Richer semantics (iterations/items/nested) are Phase 2-3 only.",
    "TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS is unchanged in Phase 1 \u2014 core/task/ lives under existing core/ package. SD-016's orchestrate/verify additions belong to Phase 4. Do not edit TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS or tests/test_doctor_setup.py.",
    "RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} in artagents/core/orchestrator/schema.py is unchanged. No new step kinds (code/attested/nested) in Phase 1.",
    "No HMAC, keyed crypto, .index.db, daemon, web server, or shared CAS \u2014 SD-029. Hash chain is plain sha256.",
    "command_for_argv produces the canonical form plan.json command strings must match exactly (including --project <slug> token). Document this contract in code comment near the helper or in the docs footnote.",

  "meta_commentary": "Phase 1 is purely additive and runs entirely in-process \u2014 no infra, secrets, or human approvals required. Execute strictly in plan order: kernel modules (T1\u2013T4) \u2192 unit tests (T5) \u2192 env-contract change with regression check (T6\u2013T7) \u2192 pipeline wiring (T8) \u2192 defensive re-checks one runner at a time (T9\u2013T10) with intermediate test runs \u2192 dispatch coverage (T11) \u2192 e2e smoke (T12) \u2192 docs (T13) \u2192 full sweep (T14). The single highest-risk file is artagents/core/project/run.py: keep the env-set branch local to prepare_project_run/finalize_project_run, never touch the env-unset code path, and re-run tests/test_project_runs.py immediately after T6 \u2014 if it fails, the env contract has leaked into the standalone path and must be reverted before proceeding. The four gate entry points all share one ordering invariant: gate runs BEFORE _prepare_project_request and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run; verify by filesystem assertion that <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ does not appear after rejection. command_for_argv must produce the exact canonical form that plan.json command strings are authored against, including the --project <slug> token; this is the contract that makes hand-authored plans work in T12. FLAG-002 stays open as documented (canonical-JSON hashing, not raw bytes); flag it in code review but do not change scope without user confirmation. Recovery strings forward-reference Phase 5 verbs that don't exist yet (artagents abort, artagents next) \u2014 emit them verbatim per SD-011, do not substitute. New env var ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is in-scope for Phase 1 (justified in plan assumptions because SD-018 mirroring needs a step id in the child); document it in the Phase 1 footnote so it is visible at Phase 5 review time.",

TASK_RUN_ID_ENV, TASK_STEP_ID_ENV\n+from artagents.packs.builtin.hype import run as hype_run\n+\n+\n+def test_task_env_prepare_project_run_attaches_to_step_dir_without_run_json(\n+    tmp_projects_root: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n+) -> None:\n+    create_project(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    _set_task_env(monkeypatch, project=\"demo\", run_id=\"task-run-1\", step_id=\"step-1\")\n+\n+    context = prepare_project_run(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+\n+    assert context.run_root == tmp_projects_root / \"demo\" / \"runs\" / \"task-run-1\" / \"steps\" / \"step-1\"\n+    assert context.run_id == \"task-run-1\"\n+    assert not context.run_json_path.exists()\n+    assert context.record[\"status\"] == \"attached\"\n+    assert context.record[\"metadata\"][\"attached_to_task_run\"] is True\n+    assert context.record[\"metadata\"][\"task_step_id\"] == \"step-1\"\n+\n+\n+def test_task_env_second_step_reuses_same_parent_run_dir(tmp_projects_root: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n+    create_project(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    _set_task_env(monkeypatch, project=\"demo\", run_id=\"task-run-1\", step_id=\"step-1\")\n+    first = prepare_project_run(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    monkeypatch.setenv(TASK_STEP_ID_ENV, \"step-2\")\n+\n+    second = prepare_project_run(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+\n+    assert first.run_root.parent == second.run_root.parent\n+    assert second.run_root == tmp_projects_root / \"demo\" / \"runs\" / \"task-run-1\" / \"steps\" / \"step-2\"\n+\n+\n+def test_task_env_project_mismatch_rejects(tmp_projects_root: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n+    create_project(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    _set_task_env(monkeypatch, project=\"other\", run_id=\"task-run-1\", step_id=\"step-1\")\n+\n+    with pytest.raises(ProjectRunError, match=\"task run is bound to project 'other'\"):\n+        prepare_project_run(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+\n+\n+def test_task_env_missing_step_id_rejects(tmp_projects_root: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n+    create_project(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    monkeypatch.setenv(TASK_RUN_ID_ENV, \"task-run-1\")\n+    monkeypatch.setenv(TASK_PROJECT_ENV, \"demo\")\n+    monkeypatch.delenv(TASK_STEP_ID_ENV, raising=False)\n+\n+    with pytest.raises(ProjectRunError, match=\"ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID must be set\"):\n+        prepare_project_run(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+\n+\n+def test_env_unset_standalone_path_still_writes_run_json(tmp_projects_root: Path) -> None:\n+    create_project(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+\n+    context = prepare_project_run(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root, run_id=\"standalone-run\")\n+\n+    assert context.run_root == tmp_projects_root / \"demo\" / \"runs\" / \"standalone-run\"\n+    assert context.run_json_path.is_file()\n+    assert context.record[\"status\"] == \"prepared\"\n+\n+\n+def test_attached_hype_artifacts_mirror_under_step_produces(tmp_projects_root: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n+    create_project(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    _set_task_env(monkeypatch, project=\"demo\", run_id=\"task-run-1\", step_id=\"step-1\")\n+    context = prepare_project_run(\"demo\", root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    artifact_root = tmp_projects_root / \"artifacts\"\n+    artifact_root.mkdir()\n+    for name in (\"hype.timeline.json\", \"hype.assets.json\", \"hype.metadata.json\"):\n+        (artifact_root / name).write_text(\"{}\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+\n+    record = finalize_project_run(context, status=\"success\", artifact_roots=[artifact_root])\n+\n+    assert not context.run_json_path.exists()\n+    produces = context.run_root / \"produces\"\n+    assert sorted(path.name for path in produces.iterdir()) == test_prepare_project_run_is_central_chokepoint_for_project_callers(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n+    calls: list[tuple[str, str | None]] = []\n+\n+    def fake_prepare(project_slug: str, **kwargs: object) -> ProjectRunContext:\n+        calls.append((project_slug, kwargs.get(\"kind\")))\n+        run_root = tmp_path / f\"run-{len(calls)}\"\n+        return ProjectRunContext(\n+            project_slug=project_slug,\n+            run_id=f\"run-{len(calls)}\",\n+            run_root=run_root,\n+            run_json_path=run_root / \"run.json\",\n+            record={\"metadata\": {}},\n+            root=tmp_path,\n+        )\n+\n+    monkeypatch.setattr(executor_runner, \"prepare_project_run\", fake_prepare)\n+ executor_runner._prepare_project_request(\n+        ExecutorRunRequest(executor_id=\"builtin.noop\", project=\"demo\"),\n+        SimpleNamespace(id=\"builtin.noop\"),\n+    )\n+    orchestrator_runner._prepare_project_request(\n+        OrchestratorRunRequest(orchestrator_id=\"builtin.hype\", project=\"demo\"),\n+        SimpleNamespace(id=\"builtin.hype\", > DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Idea:
Implement Phase 1 (Kernel) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 1 scope: events.jsonl with plain sha256 hash chain (prev_hash + canonical_event_json, no HMAC); plan.json hash pinning; gate above dispatch as a single function checking active_run.json existence + plan.json hash match + events.jsonl chain integrity + incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command; active_run.json pointer at the project root. The runner becomes dumb; the gate is decorated/composed above runner dispatch. This is additive: existing OrchestratorDefinition JSON manifests and RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. No new step kinds yet (those are Phase 2). No produces-with-checks (Phase 3). No DSL (Phase 4). No new lifecycle verbs in this phase — that comes in Phase 5; for now, exercise the kernel through existing entry points so it can be verified end-to-end. Honor-based with strong logging — accidental-edit tripwire only. Single-host. Storage paths under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/<slug>/. Add focused tests for: hash-chain append + verify; plan-hash pin + mismatch rejection; gate-rejects-non-canonical-command with the exact recovery error; gate is called from every dispatch entry point. Migration is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13) so respect the current pack architecture.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

    "Gate must run STRICTLY BEFORE _prepare_project_request() and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run() at every entry point \u2014 assert this ordering by filesystem checks (no <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ created on rejection).",
    "Recovery strings 'artagents abort --project <slug>' and 'artagents next --project <slug>' are emitted verbatim per SD-011 even though those CLI verbs do not exist until Phase 5. Do not substitute Phase-1-actionable wording.",
    "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is a NEW sibling env var introduced in Phase 1 (the design doc names only ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID). Plan justifies it because SD-018 mirroring requires a step id in the child. Keep the constant in artagents/core/task/env.py and document in the docs footnote.",

        Idea:
Implement Phase 1 (Kernel) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 1 scope: events.jsonl with plain sha256 hash chain (prev_hash + canonical_event_json, no HMAC); plan.json hash pinning; gate above dispatch as a single function checking active_run.json existence + plan.json hash match + events.jsonl chain integrity + incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command; active_run.json pointer at the project root. The runner becomes dumb; the gate is decorated/composed above runner dispatch. This is additive: existing OrchestratorDefinition JSON manifests and RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. No new step kinds yet (those are Phase 2). No produces-with-checks (Phase 3). No DSL (Phase 4). No new lifecycle verbs in this phase — that comes in Phase 5; for now, exercise the kernel through existing entry points so it can be verified end-to-end. Honor-based with strong logging — accidental-edit tripwire only. Single-host. Storage paths under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/<slug>/. Add focused tests for: hash-chain append + verify; plan-hash pin + mismatch rejection; gate-rejects-non-canonical-command with the exact recovery error; gate is called from every dispatch entry point. Migration is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13) so respect the current pack architecture.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

  "watch_items": [
    "FLAG-002 (open, minor): plan_hash uses canonical-JSON re-serialization and tolerates whitespace-only edits to plan.json. Plan accepts this per assumption #2; if the executor finds raw-byte hashing trivial to switch to (sha256 of file bytes), prefer it for a strictest accidental-edit tripwire \u2014 but do not block on this without confirming with user.",
    "Gate must run STRICTLY BEFORE _prepare_project_request() and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run() at every entry point \u2014 assert this ordering by filesystem checks (no <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ created on rejection).",
    "Recovery strings 'artagents abort --project <slug>' and 'artagents next --project <slug>' are emitted verbatim per SD-011 even though those CLI verbs do not exist until Phase 5. Do not substitute Phase-1-actionable wording.",
    "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is a NEW sibling env var introduced in Phase 1 (the design doc names only ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID). Plan justifies it because SD-018 mirroring requires a step id in the child. Keep the constant in artagents/core/task/env.py and document in the docs footnote.",
    "Step retries with the same step_id reuse <step_dir>/ flat (mkdir parents=True, exist_ok=True). Iterations directory is Phase 3 scope; do not introduce <step_dir>/iterations/NNN/ in Phase 1.",
    "is_in_task_run(slug) must guard on BOTH TASK_RUN_ID_ENV being set AND (slug is None OR matches TASK_PROJECT_ENV). The slug-mismatch check inside prepare_project_run is a redundant defense for direct callers that bypass the gate.",
    "When attached_to_task_run is truthy, finalize_project_run must NOT write child run.json and must NOT upsert the cumulative runs.json. mirror_hype_artifacts must still run; verify it lands files under <step_dir>/produces/ rather than a sibling child runs/<id>/.",
    "tests/test_project_runs.py must pass WITHOUT EDITS \u2014 env-unset code path must be byte-identical to today.",
    "Inactive gate must be fully transparent: no active_run.json + no ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID + no --project \u21d2 GateDecision(active=False) and ZERO events written. Confirm in tests.",
    "Cursor derivation in Phase 1 = count(step_completed events). Richer semantics (iterations/items/nested) are Phase 2-3 only.",
    "TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS is unchanged in Phase 1 \u2014 core/task/ lives under existing core/ package. SD-016's orchestrate/verify additions belong to Phase 4. Do not edit TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS or tests/test_doctor_setup.py.",
    "RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} in artagents/core/orchestrator/schema.py is unchanged. No new step kinds (code/attested/nested) in Phase 1.",
    "No HMAC, keyed crypto, .index.db, daemon, web server, or shared CAS \u2014 SD-029. Hash chain is plain sha256.",
    "command_for_argv produces the canonical form plan.json command strings must match exactly (including --project <slug> token). Document this contract in code comment near the helper or in the docs footnote.",
    "Subprocess env propagation into child code-step launchers is Phase 2 scope; Phase 1 only ships the child_subprocess_env() helper used directly in the e2e smoke."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "Phase 1 is purely additive and runs entirely in-process \u2014 no infra, secrets, or human approvals required. Execute strictly in plan order: kernel modules (T1\u2013T4) \u2192 unit tests (T5) \u2192 env-contract change with regression check (T6\u2013T7) \u2192 pipeline wiring (T8) \u2192 defensive re-checks one runner at a time (T9\u2013T10) with intermediate test runs \u2192 dispatch coverage (T11) \u2192 e2e smoke (T12) \u2192 docs (T13) \u2192 full sweep (T14). The single highest-risk file is artagents/core/project/run.py: keep the env-set branch local to prepare_project_run/finalize_project_run, never touch the env-unset code path, and re-run tests/test_project_runs.py immediately after T6 \u2014 if it fails, the env contract has leaked into the standalone path and must be reverted before proceeding. The four gate entry points all share one ordering invariant: gate runs BEFORE _prepare_project_request and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run; verify by filesystem assertion that <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ does not appear after rejection. command_for_argv must produce the exact canonical form that plan.json command strings are authored against, including the --project <slug> token; this is the contract that makes hand-authored plans work in T12. FLAG-002 stays open as documented (canonical-JSON hashing, not raw bytes); flag it in code review but do not change scope without user confirmation. Recovery strings forward-reference Phase 5 verbs that don't exist yet (artagents abort, artagents next) \u2014 emit them verbatim per SD-011, do not substitute. New env var ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is in-scope for Phase 1 (justified in plan assumptions because SD-018 mirroring needs a step id in the child); document it in the Phase 1 footnote so it is visible at Phase 5 review time.",

        Idea:
Implement Phase 1 (Kernel) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 1 scope: events.jsonl with plain sha256 hash chain (prev_hash + canonical_event_json, no HMAC); plan.json hash pinning; gate above dispatch as a single function checking active_run.json existence + plan.json hash match + events.jsonl chain integrity + incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command; active_run.json pointer at the project root. The runner becomes dumb; the gate is decorated/composed above runner dispatch. This is additive: existing OrchestratorDefinition JSON manifests and RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command} keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. No new step kinds yet (those are Phase 2). No produces-with-checks (Phase 3). No DSL (Phase 4). No new lifecycle verbs in this phase — that comes in Phase 5; for now, exercise the kernel through existing entry points so it can be verified end-to-end. Honor-based with strong logging — accidental-edit tripwire only. Single-host. Storage paths under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/<slug>/. Add focused tests for: hash-chain append + verify; plan-hash pin + mismatch rejection; gate-rejects-non-canonical-command with the exact recovery error; gate is called from every dispatch entry point. Migration is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13) so respect the current pack architecture.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

    "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is a NEW sibling env var introduced in Phase 1 (the design doc names only ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID). Plan justifies it because SD-018 mirroring requires a step id in the child. Keep the constant in artagents/core/task/env.py and document in the docs footnote.",
    "Step retries with the same step_id reuse <step_dir>/ flat (mkdir parents=True, exist_ok=True). Iterations directory is Phase 3 scope; do not introduce <step_dir>/iterations/NNN/ in Phase 1.",

    "Inactive gate must be fully transparent: no active_run.json + no ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID + no --project \u21d2 GateDecision(active=False) and ZERO events written. Confirm in tests.",

  "meta_commentary": "Phase 1 is purely additive and runs entirely in-process \u2014 no infra, secrets, or human approvals required. Execute strictly in plan order: kernel modules (T1\u2013T4) \u2192 unit tests (T5) \u2192 env-contract change with regression check (T6\u2013T7) \u2192 pipeline wiring (T8) \u2192 defensive re-checks one runner at a time (T9\u2013T10) with intermediate test runs \u2192 dispatch coverage (T11) \u2192 e2e smoke (T12) \u2192 docs (T13) \u2192 full sweep (T14). The single highest-risk file is artagents/core/project/run.py: keep the env-set branch local to prepare_project_run/finalize_project_run, never touch the env-unset code path, and re-run tests/test_project_runs.py immediately after T6 \u2014 if it fails, the env contract has leaked into the standalone path and must be reverted before proceeding. The four gate entry points all share one ordering invariant: gate runs BEFORE _prepare_project_request and thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run; verify by filesystem assertion that <projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<id>/ does not appear after rejection. command_for_argv must produce the exact canonical form that plan.json command strings are authored against, including the --project <slug> token; this is the contract that makes hand-authored plans work in T12. FLAG-002 stays open as documented (canonical-JSON hashing, not raw bytes); flag it in code review but do not change scope without user confirmation. Recovery strings forward-reference Phase 5 verbs that don't exist yet (artagents abort, artagents next) \u2014 emit them verbatim per SD-011, do not substitute. New env var ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID is in-scope for Phase 1 (justified in plan assumptions because SD-018 mirroring needs a step id in the child); document it in the Phase 1 footnote so it is visible at Phase 5 review time.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked v5's worker auth story against `/users/user_c042661f/documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py` and `worker.py`. `worker_jwt.verify_user_jwt` validates signature/audience/expiry and returns the jwt subject; it does not prove project or timeline ownership. the reference worker separately calls `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)` via a service-role read of `projects.user_id` before writing. v5 says `worker_jwt.py` confirms the user owns the timeline and only tests missing ownership claims, but it does not add the separate project-ownership check, which is required when the terminal write uses service-role. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] auth-scope: auth scope: v5 broadens service-role timeline writes from the worker-only path into generic supabasedataprovider, open_in_reigh, and project cli edit flows, conflicting with sd-009's user-jwt/pat ownership-bound cli model. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements: did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked v5 against sd-009 and the corrected user notes. the corrected facts only establish the service-role rpc path for the trusted aa worker, but v5 makes the generic `supabasedataprovider.save_timeline`, `open_in_reigh` default flow, and project cli edit verbs all take `service_role_key` and call the versioned rpc. that broadens service-role use beyond the worker and conflicts with the brief's user-jwt/pat ownership-bound cli path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked the step 1 source-reference paths for the local packages. the package manifests live at the package roots, but the source files are under `typescript/src/index.ts`; `packages/timeline-ops/src/index.ts` and `packages/timeline-schema/src/index.ts` do not exist. v5 says to read `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops/{package.json,src/index.ts}` and the matching schema path, so that documentation step still points at missing source files even though the install targets are fixed. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the reference worker call chain for write authorization. in `banodoco-worker/worker.py`, after jwt verification the caller creates `supabasetimelinerpc(audited_user_id=verified.user_id)` and then calls `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)` before the service-role write. v5's worker caller path lacks that explicit ownership-verification call and relies on `worker_jwt.py` to prove ownership, which the reference `worker_jwt.py` does not do. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] package-source-path-references: package source path references: v5 fixes package-root file dependencies, but phase 0 step 1 still names package-root `src/index.ts` files that do not exist; the actual source files are under `typescript/src/index.ts`. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
